= MOMENT - MOUTHS
:toc:

== MOMENT

* `Exo 33:5` For the LORD had said to Moses, "Say to the children of Israel, 'You are a stiff-necked people. I will come up into the midst of you in a *_moment_* and consume you. And now, take off your ornaments from you so that I may know what to do to you.'"
* `Num 16:21` "Separate yourselves from among this congregation so that I may consume them in a *_moment_*."
* `Num 16:45` "Get away from among this congregation so that I may consume them as in a *_moment_*." And they fell upon their faces.
* `Ezr 9:8` And now for a brief *_moment_*, grace has been shown from the LORD our God, to leave us a remnant to escape and to give us a nail in his holy place, so that our God may lighten our eyes and give us a little reviving in our bondage.
* `Job 7:18` And that you should visit him every morning and try him every *_moment_*?
* `Job 20:5` That the triumphing of the wicked is short, and the joy of the hypocrite is only for a *_moment_*?
* `Job 21:13` They spend their days in wealth and in a *_moment_* go down to the grave.
* `Job 34:20` In a *_moment_* they shall die. And the people shall be troubled at midnight and pass away. And the mighty shall be taken away without the hand [of men].
* `Psa 30:5` For his anger endures only a *_moment_*. In his favor is life. Weeping may endure for a night, but joy comes in the morning.
* `Psa 73:19` How they are brought into desolation in a *_moment_*! They are utterly consumed with terrors.
* `Pro 12:19` The lip of truth shall be established forever, but a lying tongue is only for a *_moment_*.
* `Isa 27:3` I, the LORD, keep it. I will water it every *_moment_*. Lest any hurt it, I will keep it night and day.
* `Isa 47:9` But these two things shall come to you in a *_moment_* in one day: the loss of children and widowhood. They shall come upon you in their perfection in spite of the multitude of your sorceries and in spite of the great abundance of your enchantments.
* `Isa 54:7` "For a small *_moment_* I have forsaken you, but with great mercies I will gather you.
* `Isa 54:8` In a little wrath I hid my face from you for a *_moment_*, but with everlasting kindness I will have mercy on you," says the LORD your Redeemer.
* `Jer 4:20` Destruction upon destruction is cried, for the whole land is laid waste. Suddenly my tents are ruined, and my curtains in a *_moment_*.
* `Lam 4:6` [VAV] For the punishment of the iniquity of the daughter of my people is greater than the punishment of the sin of Sodom, which was overthrown as in a *_moment_*, and no hands stayed on her.
* `Ezk 26:16` Then all the princes of the sea shall come down from their thrones, lay away their robes, and take off their embroidered garments. They shall clothe themselves with trembling. They shall sit upon the ground and shall tremble at every *_moment_* and be astonished at you.
* `Ezk 32:10` Indeed, I will make many people amazed at you, and their kings shall be horribly afraid for you when I brandish my sword before them. And they shall tremble at every *_moment_*, every man for his own life, in the day of your fall."
* `Luk 4:5` And the devil taking him up upon a high mountain showed to him all the kingdoms of the world in a *_moment_* of time.
* `1Co 15:52` In a *_moment_*, in the twinkling of an eye, at the last trumpet. For the trumpet shall sound, the dead shall be raised incorruptible, and we shall be changed.
* `2Co 4:17` For our light affliction, which is only for a *_moment_*, works out for us a far more exceeding and eternal weight of glory,

* `Jer 44:25` Thus says the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel, saying, 'You and your wives have both spoken with your *_mouths_* and fulfilled with your hand, saying, "We will surely perform our vows that we have vowed, to burn incense to the queen of heaven and to pour out drink-offerings to her." You will surely accomplish your vows and surely perform your vows.'
* `Lam 3:46` [AYIN] All our enemies have opened their *_mouths_* against us.
* `Dan 6:22` My God has sent his angel and has shut the lions' *_mouths_*, and they have not hurt me, because before him innocence was found in me. And also before you, O king, I have done no hurt."
* `Mic 3:5` Thus says the LORD concerning the prophets who deceive my people, who bite with their teeth, and cry, "Peace," and he who puts nothing into their *_mouths_*, they even prepare war against him:
* `Tit 1:11` Their *_mouths_* must be stopped, seeing that they subvert whole households, teaching things which they ought not for the sake of sordid gain.
* `Heb 11:33` Who through faith subdued kingdoms, wrought righteousness, obtained promises, stopped the *_mouths_* of lions,
* `Jms 3:3` Behold, we put bits in the *_mouths_* of horses so that they may obey us, and we direct their whole body.
* `Rev 9:17` And thus I saw the horses in the vision and those who sat on them, having breast-plates of fire, of jacinth, and brimstone. And the heads of the horses were like the heads of lions, and out of their *_mouths_* issued fire, smoke, and brimstone.
* `Rev 9:18` By these three the third part of men was killed: by the fire, by the smoke, and by the brimstone which issued out of their *_mouths_*.

_18 verses containing MOUTHS_
